#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;

// Index calculation for flattened 2D array
inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
    return j * n + i;
}

void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes, 
                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);
    }
    
    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;
    }
}

void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            path[idx2(i, j, numNodes)] = j;
            path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = i;
        }
        path[idx2(j, j, numNodes)] = j;
    }
}

void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                   const size_t numNodes) {
    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
    // For each intermediate node k
    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
        // For each source node i
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            // For each destination node j
            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;
                
                if (newDist < distIJ) {
                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {
    // Basic sanity checks
    
    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {
            printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\n", i, i);
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]
    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time
    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {
            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                // Check for overflow before addition
                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {
                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {
                        printf("Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\n", 
                               i, j, k);
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t numNodes = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of nodes: %zu\n", numNodes);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(numNodes * numNodes);
    std::vector<unsigned int> path(numNodes * numNodes);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing graph...\n");
    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);
    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes);
    
    // Run Floyd-Warshall
    printf("Computing shortest paths...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate operations per second
    // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity
    double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation (integer hash-based)
    if (printResults) {
        print_results_int(dist, "DistanceMatrix");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(dist, numNodes);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;

// Index calculation for flattened 2D array
inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
    return j * n + i;
}

void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes, 
                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);
    }
    
    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;
    }
}

void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            path[idx2(i, j, numNodes)] = j;
            path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = i;
        }
        path[idx2(j, j, numNodes)] = j;
    }
}

void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                   const size_t numNodes) {
    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
    // For each intermediate node k
    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
        // For each source node i
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            // For each destination node j
            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;
                
                if (newDist < distIJ) {
                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {
    // Basic sanity checks
    
    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {
            printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\n", i, i);
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]
    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time
    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {
            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                // Check for overflow before addition
                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {
                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {
                        printf("Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\n", 
                               i, j, k);
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t numNodes = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of nodes: %zu\n", numNodes);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(numNodes * numNodes);
    std::vector<unsigned int> path(numNodes * numNodes);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing graph...\n");
    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);
    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes);
    
    // Run Floyd-Warshall
    printf("Computing shortest paths...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate operations per second
    // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity
    double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation (integer hash-based)
    if (printResults) {
        print_results_int(dist, "DistanceMatrix");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(dist, numNodes);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

/bin/bash -lc 'cat floydwarshall/floydwarshall.cpp' in /home/llmtest/evals/20261006-204623/floydwarshall_gpt-6.1-sol-xhigh_omp_r1

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;

// Index calculation for flattened 2D array
inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
    return j * n + i;
}

void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes, 
                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);
    }
    
    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;
    }
}

void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            path[idx2(i, j, numNodes)] = j;
            path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = i;
        }
        path[idx2(j, j, numNodes)] = j;
    }
}

void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                   const size_t numNodes) {
    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
    // For each intermediate node k
    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
        // For each source node i
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            // For each destination node j
            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;
                
                if (newDist < distIJ) {
                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {
    // Basic sanity checks
    
    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {
            printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\n", i, i);
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]
    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time
    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {
            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                // Check for overflow before addition
                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {
                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {
                        printf("Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\n", 
                               i, j, k);
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t numNodes = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of nodes: %zu\n", numNodes);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(numNodes * numNodes);
    std::vector<unsigned int> path(numNodes * numNodes);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing graph...\n");
    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);
    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes);
    
    // Run Floyd-Warshall
    printf("Computing shortest paths...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate operations per second
    // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity
    double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation (integer hash-based)
    if (printResults) {
        print_results_int(dist, "DistanceMatrix");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(dist, numNodes);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

for n in (33, 65, 257, 512, 1024):
    expected = None
    for name, (binary, fd) in binaries.items():
        for threads in ((1,) if name == \"original\" else (1, 4, 16)):
            env = dict(os.environ, OMP_NUM_THREADS=str(threads), OMP_PROC_BIND=\"close\", OMP_PLACES=\"cores\")
            output = subprocess.run([binary, \"-n\", str(n), \"-r\", \"-v\"], text=True, capture_output=True, check=True, pass_fds=(fd,), env=env).stdout
            result = output.split(\"=== RESULTS ===\")[1].split(\"=== END RESULTS ===\")[0]
            if expected is None: expected = result
            assert expected == result, (name, n, threads)
            elapsed = re.search(r\"Computation time: (\\d+) ms\", output).group(1)
            print(f\"n={n} {name:8} threads={threads:2} time={elapsed:>5} ms exact distance hash matched\", flush=True)

for n in (33, 65, 257, 512, 1024):
    expected = None
    for name, (binary, fd) in binaries.items():
        for threads in ((1,) if name == \"original\" else (1, 4, 16)):
            env = dict(os.environ, OMP_NUM_THREADS=str(threads), OMP_PROC_BIND=\"close\", OMP_PLACES=\"cores\")
            output = subprocess.run([binary, \"-n\", str(n), \"-r\", \"-v\"], text=True, capture_output=True, check=True, pass_fds=(fd,), env=env).stdout
            result = output.split(\"=== RESULTS ===\")[1].split(\"=== END RESULTS ===\")[0]
            if expected is None: expected = result
            assert expected == result, (name, n, threads)
            elapsed = re.search(r\"Computation time: (\\d+) ms\", output).group(1)
            print(f\"n={n} {name:8} threads={threads:2} time={elapsed:>5} ms exact distance hash matched\", flush=True)

The first versions match the original distance hashes at several graph sizes. Row parallelism is faster so far. I’m comparing SIMD update strategies and larger graphs before choosing the final version, and I’ll also compare every path entry to check tie behavior.

codex
The initial cache tiling costs more than it saves on graphs up to 2,048 nodes. I’m testing one further optimization: skipping tiles whose largest distance proves that the current pivot cannot improve any entry. This keeps the strict comparison used for path updates.

        for name, (binary, fd) in binaries.items():
            output = subprocess.run([binary, \"-n\", str(n), \"-r\", \"-v\"], text=True, capture_output=True, check=True, pass_fds=(fd,), env=env).stdout

            if expected is None: expected = result
            assert expected == result, (name, n, threads)
            elapsed = re.search(r\"Computation time: (\\d+) ms\", output).group(1)
            print(f\"n={n} {name:11} threads={threads:2} time={elapsed:>5} ms\", flush=True)

for n in (512, 1024, 2048):
    expected = None
    for threads in (1, 8, 16, 32, 64):
        env = dict(os.environ, OMP_NUM_THREADS=str(threads), OMP_PROC_BIND=\"close\", OMP_PLACES=\"cores\")
        for name, (binary, fd) in binaries.items():
            if name == \"rows\" and threads not in (1, 32): continue
            times = []
            for repeat in range(2):
                output = subprocess.run([binary, \"-n\", str(n), \"-r\", \"-v\"], text=True, capture_output=True, check=True, pass_fds=(fd,), env=env).stdout
                result = output.split(\"=== RESULTS ===\")[1].split(\"=== END RESULTS ===\")[0]
                if expected is None: expected = result
                assert expected == result, (name, n, threads)
                times.append(int(re.search(r\"Computation time: (\\d+) ms\", output).group(1)))
            print(f\"n={n} {name:8} threads={threads:2} time={statistics.median(times):7.1f} ms\", flush=True)

The tile-skipping version is also slower on this workload. I’m narrowing the implementation to one persistent OpenMP team, independent row updates, and a SIMD loop that preserves path entries on ties. I’m checking this version against the original on random, disconnected, zero-weight, and equal-distance graphs.

codex
The fastest version batches 16 pivots and saves each pivot row at the point where the original algorithm would use it. Workers can then update whole rows without a barrier after every pivot. At 2,048 nodes with 32 threads, this measured about 84 ms, versus 95 ms for straightforward row parallelism. I’m applying that version and verifying it through CMake.

 #include <cstring>

+#include <omp.h>

 #include "../common/results_output.hpp"
 
 constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
 constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;
+constexpr size_t FW_BLOCK_SIZE = 16;
 
+inline int matrixThreadCount(const size_t numNodes) noexcept {
+    const size_t blocks = numNodes / FW_BLOCK_SIZE + (numNodes % FW_BLOCK_SIZE != 0);
+    // The pivot-row phase has one independent column tile per block. Avoid
+    // starting more workers than that phase can use, especially on small graphs.
+    return static_cast<int>(std::min(static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()),
+                                     std::max(size_t{1}, blocks)));
+}
+
 // Index calculation for flattened 2D array
 inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
     return j * n + i;

+    const int numThreads = matrixThreadCount(numNodes);
+    #pragma omp parallel for num_threads(numThreads) schedule(static) default(none) shared(path, numNodes)
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
+        // Both assignments in the original initialization set each entry to
+        // its source row. Fill each row once, with contiguous stores.
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
+            path[i * numNodes + j] = static_cast<unsigned int>(i);

+inline void relaxRow(unsigned int* row, unsigned int* pathRow,
+                     const unsigned int* pivot, const unsigned int distanceIK,
+                     const unsigned int k, const size_t count) {
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < count; ++j) {
+        const unsigned int candidate = distanceIK + pivot[j];
+        if (candidate < row[j]) {
+            row[j] = candidate;
+            pathRow[j] = k;
+        }
+    }

+    const int numThreads = matrixThreadCount(numNodes);
+    // Keep the pivot rows as they appeared at their individual k iterations.
+    // Using their final values after an ordinary blocked closure would change
+    // the original strict-improvement path entries, even with equal distances.
+    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotStorage(FW_BLOCK_SIZE * numNodes);
+    unsigned int pivotColumns[FW_BLOCK_SIZE * FW_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    unsigned int* distances = dist.data();
+    unsigned int* paths = path.data();

+    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(numThreads) default(none) shared(distances, paths, pivots, pivotColumns, numNodes, numBlocks)
+    {
+        for (size_t block = 0; block < numBlocks; ++block) {
+            const size_t begin = block * FW_BLOCK_SIZE;
+            const size_t width = std::min(FW_BLOCK_SIZE, numNodes - begin);
+
+            // First process the small diagonal tile in the original k order,
+            // saving both the pivot row and source-to-pivot column at each k.
+            #pragma omp single
+            {
+                for (size_t offset = 0; offset < width; ++offset) {
+                    const size_t k = begin + offset;
+                    const unsigned int* pivot = distances + k * numNodes;
+                    unsigned int* snapshot = pivots + offset * numNodes;
+                    std::copy_n(pivot + begin, width, snapshot + begin);
+                    for (size_t rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < width; ++rowOffset) {
+                        pivotColumns[offset * FW_BLOCK_SIZE + rowOffset] =
+                            distances[(begin + rowOffset) * numNodes + k];
+                    }
+                    for (size_t rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < width; ++rowOffset) {
+                        // A zero diagonal makes the pivot row unchanged at k.
+                        if (rowOffset == offset) continue;
+                        const size_t i = begin + rowOffset;
+                        relaxRow(distances + i * numNodes + begin,
+                                 paths + i * numNodes + begin, snapshot + begin,
+                                 pivotColumns[offset * FW_BLOCK_SIZE + rowOffset],
+                                 static_cast<unsigned int>(k), width);
+                    }
                 }
             }

+            // Each worker owns disjoint columns of the pivot rows. Complete
+            // their updates and snapshots without any per-pivot barriers.
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t columnBlock = 0; columnBlock < numBlocks; ++columnBlock) {
+                if (columnBlock == block) continue;
+                const size_t column = columnBlock * FW_BLOCK_SIZE;
+                const size_t count = std::min(FW_BLOCK_SIZE, numNodes - column);
+                for (size_t offset = 0; offset < width; ++offset) {
+                    const size_t k = begin + offset;

+                    std::copy_n(distances + k * numNodes + column, count, snapshot);

+                        if (rowOffset == offset) continue;

+                        relaxRow(distances + i * numNodes + column,
+                                 paths + i * numNodes + column, snapshot,
+                                 pivotColumns[offset * FW_BLOCK_SIZE + rowOffset],
+                                 static_cast<unsigned int>(k), count);
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+
+            // The barrier above publishes every immutable pivot snapshot.
+            // Other rows are now independent: retain each row in cache while
+            // applying all pivots in order, preserving distances and paths.
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
+                if (i >= begin && i < begin + width) continue;
+                unsigned int* row = distances + i * numNodes;
+                unsigned int* pathRow = paths + i * numNodes;
+                for (size_t offset = 0; offset < width; ++offset) {
+                    const size_t k = begin + offset;
+                    relaxRow(row, pathRow, pivots + offset * numNodes,
+                             row[k], static_cast<unsigned int>(k), numNodes);
+                }
+            }
+            // All rows must finish before the next block reads its pivots.
         }
     }
 }

 #include <cstring>
 #include <vector>
 
+#include <omp.h>
+
 #include "../common/results_output.hpp"
 
 constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
 constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;
+constexpr size_t FW_BLOCK_SIZE = 16;
 
+inline int matrixThreadCount(const size_t numNodes) noexcept {
+    const size_t blocks = numNodes / FW_BLOCK_SIZE + (numNodes % FW_BLOCK_SIZE != 0);
+    // The pivot-row phase has one independent column tile per block. Avoid
+    // starting more workers than that phase can use, especially on small graphs.
+    return static_cast<int>(std::min(static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()),
+                                     std::max(size_t{1}, blocks)));
+}
+
 // Index calculation for flattened 2D array
 inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
     return j * n + i;
@@ -32,36 +43,113 @@
 }
 
 void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
-    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
-            path[idx2(i, j, numNodes)] = j;
-            path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = i;
+    const int numThreads = matrixThreadCount(numNodes);
+    #pragma omp parallel for num_threads(numThreads) schedule(static) default(none) shared(path, numNodes)
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
+        // Both assignments in the original initialization set each entry to
+        // its source row. Fill each row once, with contiguous stores.
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {

+inline void relaxRow(unsigned int* row, unsigned int* pathRow,
+                     const unsigned int* pivot, const unsigned int distanceIK,
+                     const unsigned int k, const size_t count) {
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < count; ++j) {
+        const unsigned int candidate = distanceIK + pivot[j];
+        if (candidate < row[j]) {
+            row[j] = candidate;
+            pathRow[j] = k;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
 void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
                    std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                    const size_t numNodes) {
-    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
-    // For each intermediate node k
-    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
-        // For each source node i
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
-            // For each destination node j
-            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
-                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
-                
-                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;
-                
-                if (newDist < distIJ) {
-                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
-                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
+    const size_t numBlocks = numNodes / FW_BLOCK_SIZE + (numNodes % FW_BLOCK_SIZE != 0);
+    const int numThreads = matrixThreadCount(numNodes);
+    // Keep the pivot rows as they appeared at their individual k iterations.
+    // Using their final values after an ordinary blocked closure would change
+    // the original strict-improvement path entries, even with equal distances.
+    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotStorage(FW_BLOCK_SIZE * numNodes);
+    unsigned int pivotColumns[FW_BLOCK_SIZE * FW_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    unsigned int* distances = dist.data();
+    unsigned int* paths = path.data();
+    unsigned int* pivots = pivotStorage.data();
+
+    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(numThreads) default(none) shared(distances, paths, pivots, pivotColumns, numNodes, numBlocks)
+    {
+        for (size_t block = 0; block < numBlocks; ++block) {
+            const size_t begin = block * FW_BLOCK_SIZE;
+            const size_t width = std::min(FW_BLOCK_SIZE, numNodes - begin);
+
+            // First process the small diagonal tile in the original k order,
+            // saving both the pivot row and source-to-pivot column at each k.
+            #pragma omp single
+            {
+                for (size_t offset = 0; offset < width; ++offset) {
+                    const size_t k = begin + offset;
+                    const unsigned int* pivot = distances + k * numNodes;
+                    unsigned int* snapshot = pivots + offset * numNodes;
+                    std::copy_n(pivot + begin, width, snapshot + begin);
+                    for (size_t rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < width; ++rowOffset) {
+                        pivotColumns[offset * FW_BLOCK_SIZE + rowOffset] =
+                            distances[(begin + rowOffset) * numNodes + k];
+                    }
+                    for (size_t rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < width; ++rowOffset) {
+                        // A zero diagonal makes the pivot row unchanged at k.
+                        if (rowOffset == offset) continue;
+                        const size_t i = begin + rowOffset;
+                        relaxRow(distances + i * numNodes + begin,
+                                 paths + i * numNodes + begin, snapshot + begin,
+                                 pivotColumns[offset * FW_BLOCK_SIZE + rowOffset],
+                                 static_cast<unsigned int>(k), width);
+                    }
                 }
             }
+
+            // Each worker owns disjoint columns of the pivot rows. Complete
+            // their updates and snapshots without any per-pivot barriers.
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t columnBlock = 0; columnBlock < numBlocks; ++columnBlock) {
+                if (columnBlock == block) continue;
+                const size_t column = columnBlock * FW_BLOCK_SIZE;
+                const size_t count = std::min(FW_BLOCK_SIZE, numNodes - column);
+                for (size_t offset = 0; offset < width; ++offset) {
+                    const size_t k = begin + offset;
+                    unsigned int* snapshot = pivots + offset * numNodes + column;
+                    std::copy_n(distances + k * numNodes + column, count, snapshot);
+                    for (size_t rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < width; ++rowOffset) {
+                        if (rowOffset == offset) continue;
+                        const size_t i = begin + rowOffset;
+                        relaxRow(distances + i * numNodes + column,
+                                 paths + i * numNodes + column, snapshot,
+                                 pivotColumns[offset * FW_BLOCK_SIZE + rowOffset],
+                                 static_cast<unsigned int>(k), count);
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+
+            // The barrier above publishes every immutable pivot snapshot.
+            // Other rows are now independent: retain each row in cache while
+            // applying all pivots in order, preserving distances and paths.
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
+                if (i >= begin && i < begin + width) continue;
+                unsigned int* row = distances + i * numNodes;
+                unsigned int* pathRow = paths + i * numNodes;
+                for (size_t offset = 0; offset < width; ++offset) {
+                    const size_t k = begin + offset;
+                    relaxRow(row, pathRow, pivots + offset * numNodes,
+                             row[k], static_cast<unsigned int>(k), numNodes);
+                }
+            }
+            // All rows must finish before the next block reads its pivots.
         }
     }
 }

+                    for (size_t rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < width; ++rowOffset) {
+                        // A zero diagonal makes the pivot row unchanged at k.
+                        if (rowOffset == offset) continue;
+                        const size_t i = begin + rowOffset;
+                        relaxRow(distances + i * numNodes + begin,
+                                 paths + i * numNodes + begin, snapshot + begin,
+                                 pivotColumns[offset * FW_BLOCK_SIZE + rowOffset],
+                                 static_cast<unsigned int>(k), width);
+                    }
                 }
             }
+
+            // Each worker owns disjoint columns of the pivot rows. Complete
+            // their updates and snapshots without any per-pivot barriers.
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t columnBlock = 0; columnBlock < numBlocks; ++columnBlock) {
+                if (columnBlock == block) continue;
+                const size_t column = columnBlock * FW_BLOCK_SIZE;
+                const size_t count = std::min(FW_BLOCK_SIZE, numNodes - column);
+                for (size_t offset = 0; offset < width; ++offset) {
+                    const size_t k = begin + offset;
+                    unsigned int* snapshot = pivots + offset * numNodes + column;
+                    std::copy_n(distances + k * numNodes + column, count, snapshot);
+                    for (size_t rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < width; ++rowOffset) {
+                        if (rowOffset == offset) continue;
+                        const size_t i = begin + rowOffset;
+                        relaxRow(distances + i * numNodes + column,
+                                 paths + i * numNodes + column, snapshot,
+                                 pivotColumns[offset * FW_BLOCK_SIZE + rowOffset],
+                                 static_cast<unsigned int>(k), count);
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+
+            // The barrier above publishes every immutable pivot snapshot.
+            // Other rows are now independent: retain each row in cache while
+            // applying all pivots in order, preserving distances and paths.
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
+                if (i >= begin && i < begin + width) continue;
+                unsigned int* row = distances + i * numNodes;
+                unsigned int* pathRow = paths + i * numNodes;
+                for (size_t offset = 0; offset < width; ++offset) {
+                    const size_t k = begin + offset;
+                    relaxRow(row, pathRow, pivots + offset * numNodes,
+                             row[k], static_cast<unsigned int>(k), numNodes);
+                }
+            }
+            // All rows must finish before the next block reads its pivots.
         }
     }
 }

 #include <cstring>

+#include <omp.h>

 #include "../common/results_output.hpp"
 
 constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
 constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;
+constexpr size_t FW_BLOCK_SIZE = 16;
 
+inline int matrixThreadCount(const size_t numNodes) noexcept {
+    const size_t blocks = numNodes / FW_BLOCK_SIZE + (numNodes % FW_BLOCK_SIZE != 0);
+    // The pivot-row phase has one independent column tile per block. Avoid
+    // starting more workers than that phase can use, especially on small graphs.
+    return static_cast<int>(std::min(static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()),
+                                     std::max(size_t{1}, blocks)));
+}
+

+inline void relaxRow(unsigned int* row, unsigned int* pathRow,
+                     const unsigned int* pivot, const unsigned int distanceIK,
+                     const unsigned int k, const size_t count) {

+    for (size_t j = 0; j < count; ++j) {
+        const unsigned int candidate = distanceIK + pivot[j];
+        if (candidate < row[j]) {
+            row[j] = candidate;

 #include <cstring>
 #include <vector>
 
+#include <omp.h>
+
 #include "../common/results_output.hpp"
 
 constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
 constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;
+constexpr size_t FW_BLOCK_SIZE = 16;
 
+inline int matrixThreadCount(const size_t numNodes) noexcept {
+    const size_t blocks = numNodes / FW_BLOCK_SIZE + (numNodes % FW_BLOCK_SIZE != 0);
+    // The pivot-row phase has one independent column tile per block. Avoid
+    // starting more workers than that phase can use, especially on small graphs.
+    return static_cast<int>(std::min(static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()),
+                                     std::max(size_t{1}, blocks)));
+}
+

     return j * n + i;

+inline void relaxRow(unsigned int* row, unsigned int* pathRow,
+                     const unsigned int* pivot, const unsigned int distanceIK,
+                     const unsigned int k, const size_t count) {
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < count; ++j) {
+        const unsigned int candidate = distanceIK + pivot[j];
+        if (candidate < row[j]) {
+            row[j] = candidate;
+            pathRow[j] = k;
+        }
+    }

 void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
                    std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                    const size_t numNodes) {
-    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
-    // For each intermediate node k
-    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
-        // For each source node i
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
-            // For each destination node j
-            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
-                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];

-                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
+    const size_t numBlocks = numNodes / FW_BLOCK_SIZE + (numNodes % FW_BLOCK_SIZE != 0);
+    const int numThreads = matrixThreadCount(numNodes);
+    // Keep the pivot rows as they appeared at their individual k iterations.
+    // Using their final values after an ordinary blocked closure would change
+    // the original strict-improvement path entries, even with equal distances.
+    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotStorage(FW_BLOCK_SIZE * numNodes);
+    unsigned int pivotColumns[FW_BLOCK_SIZE * FW_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    unsigned int* distances = dist.data();
+    unsigned int* paths = path.data();
+    unsigned int* pivots = pivotStorage.data();
+
+    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(numThreads) default(none) shared(distances, paths, pivots, pivotColumns, numNodes, numBlocks, FW_BLOCK_SIZE)
+    {
+        for (size_t block = 0; block < numBlocks; ++block) {
+            const size_t begin = block * FW_BLOCK_SIZE;
+            const size_t width = std::min(FW_BLOCK_SIZE, numNodes - begin);
+
+            // First process the small diagonal tile in the original k order,
+            // saving both the pivot row and source-to-pivot column at each k.
+            #pragma omp single
+            {
+                for (size_t offset = 0; offset < width; ++offset) {
+                    const size_t k = begin + offset;
+                    const unsigned int* pivot = distances + k * numNodes;
+                    unsigned int* snapshot = pivots + offset * numNodes;
+                    std::copy_n(pivot + begin, width, snapshot + begin);
+                    for (size_t rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < width; ++rowOffset) {
+                        pivotColumns[offset * FW_BLOCK_SIZE + rowOffset] =
+                            distances[(begin + rowOffset) * numNodes + k];
+                    }
+                    for (size_t rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < width; ++rowOffset) {
+                        // A zero diagonal makes the pivot row unchanged at k.
+                        if (rowOffset == offset) continue;
+                        const size_t i = begin + rowOffset;
+                        relaxRow(distances + i * numNodes + begin,
+                                 paths + i * numNodes + begin, snapshot + begin,
+                                 pivotColumns[offset * FW_BLOCK_SIZE + rowOffset],
+                                 static_cast<unsigned int>(k), width);
+                    }
                 }
             }
+
+            // Each worker owns disjoint columns of the pivot rows. Complete
+            // their updates and snapshots without any per-pivot barriers.
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t columnBlock = 0; columnBlock < numBlocks; ++columnBlock) {
+                if (columnBlock == block) continue;
+                const size_t column = columnBlock * FW_BLOCK_SIZE;
+                const size_t count = std::min(FW_BLOCK_SIZE, numNodes - column);
+                for (size_t offset = 0; offset < width; ++offset) {
+                    const size_t k = begin + offset;
+                    unsigned int* snapshot = pivots + offset * numNodes + column;
+                    std::copy_n(distances + k * numNodes + column, count, snapshot);
+                    for (size_t rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < width; ++rowOffset) {
+                        if (rowOffset == offset) continue;
+                        const size_t i = begin + rowOffset;
+                        relaxRow(distances + i * numNodes + column,
+                                 paths + i * numNodes + column, snapshot,
+                                 pivotColumns[offset * FW_BLOCK_SIZE + rowOffset],
+                                 static_cast<unsigned int>(k), count);
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+
+            // The barrier above publishes every immutable pivot snapshot.
+            // Other rows are now independent: retain each row in cache while
+            // applying all pivots in order, preserving distances and paths.
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
+                if (i >= begin && i < begin + width) continue;
+                unsigned int* row = distances + i * numNodes;
+                unsigned int* pathRow = paths + i * numNodes;
+                for (size_t offset = 0; offset < width; ++offset) {
+                    const size_t k = begin + offset;
+                    relaxRow(row, pathRow, pivots + offset * numNodes,
+                             row[k], static_cast<unsigned int>(k), numNodes);
+                }
+            }
+            // All rows must finish before the next block reads its pivots.
         }
     }

+            // Other rows are now independent: retain each row in cache while
+            // applying all pivots in order, preserving distances and paths.
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
+                if (i >= begin && i < begin + width) continue;
+                unsigned int* row = distances + i * numNodes;
+                unsigned int* pathRow = paths + i * numNodes;
+                for (size_t offset = 0; offset < width; ++offset) {
+                    const size_t k = begin + offset;
+                    relaxRow(row, pathRow, pivots + offset * numNodes,
+                             row[k], static_cast<unsigned int>(k), numNodes);
+                }
+            }
+            // All rows must finish before the next block reads its pivots.
         }
     }
 }

    13	constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
    14	constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;
    15	constexpr size_t FW_BLOCK_SIZE = 16;
    16	
    17	inline int matrixThreadCount(const size_t numNodes) noexcept {
    18	    const size_t blocks = numNodes / FW_BLOCK_SIZE + (numNodes % FW_BLOCK_SIZE != 0);
    19	    // The pivot-row phase has one independent column tile per block. Avoid
    20	    // starting more workers than that phase can use, especially on small graphs.
    21	    return static_cast<int>(std::min(static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()),
    22	                                     std::max(size_t{1}, blocks)));
    23	}
    24	
    25	// Index calculation for flattened 2D array
    26	inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
    27	    return j * n + i;
    28	}
    29	
    30	void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes, 
    31	                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
    32	    unsigned int seed = 42;
    33	    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;
    34	    
    35	    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {
    36	        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);
    37	    }
    38	    
    39	    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)
    40	    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
    41	        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;
    42	    }
    43	}
    44	
    45	void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
    46	    const int numThreads = matrixThreadCount(numNodes);
    47	    #pragma omp parallel for num_threads(numThreads) schedule(static) default(none) shared(path, numNodes)
    48	    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
    49	        // Both assignments in the original initialization set each entry to
    50	        // its source row. Fill each row once, with contiguous stores.
    51	        #pragma omp simd
    52	        for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
    53	            path[i * numNodes + j] = static_cast<unsigned int>(i);
    54	        }
    55	    }
    56	}
    57	
    58	inline void relaxRow(unsigned int* row, unsigned int* pathRow,
    59	                     const unsigned int* pivot, const unsigned int distanceIK,
    60	                     const unsigned int k, const size_t count) {
    61	    #pragma omp simd
    62	    for (size_t j = 0; j < count; ++j) {
    63	        const unsigned int candidate = distanceIK + pivot[j];
    64	        if (candidate < row[j]) {
    65	            row[j] = candidate;
    66	            pathRow[j] = k;
    67	        }
    68	    }
    69	}
    70	
    71	void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
    72	                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
    73	                   const size_t numNodes) {
    74	    const size_t numBlocks = numNodes / FW_BLOCK_SIZE + (numNodes % FW_BLOCK_SIZE != 0);
    75	    const int numThreads = matrixThreadCount(numNodes);
    76	    // Keep the pivot rows as they appeared at their individual k iterations.
    77	    // Using their final values after an ordinary blocked closure would change
    78	    // the original strict-improvement path entries, even with equal distances.
    79	    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotStorage(FW_BLOCK_SIZE * numNodes);
    80	    unsigned int pivotColumns[FW_BLOCK_SIZE * FW_BLOCK_SIZE];
    81	    unsigned int* distances = dist.data();
    82	    unsigned int* paths = path.data();
    83	    unsigned int* pivots = pivotStorage.data();
    84	
    85	    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(numThreads) default(none) shared(distances, paths, pivots, pivotColumns, numNodes, numBlocks, FW_BLOCK_SIZE)
    86	    {
    87	        for (size_t block = 0; block < numBlocks; ++block) {
    88	            const size_t begin = block * FW_BLOCK_SIZE;
    89	            const size_t width = std::min(FW_BLOCK_SIZE, numNodes - begin);
    90	
    91	            // First process the small diagonal tile in the original k order,
    92	            // saving both the pivot row and source-to-pivot column at each k.
    93	            #pragma omp single
    94	            {
    95	                for (size_t offset = 0; offset < width; ++offset) {
    96	                    const size_t k = begin + offset;
    97	                    const unsigned int* pivot = distances + k * numNodes;
    98	                    unsigned int* snapshot = pivots + offset * numNodes;
    99	                    std::copy_n(pivot + begin, width, snapshot + begin);
   100	                    for (size_t rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < width; ++rowOffset) {
   101	                        pivotColumns[offset * FW_BLOCK_SIZE + rowOffset] =
   102	                            distances[(begin + rowOffset) * numNodes + k];
   103	                    }
   104	                    for (size_t rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < width; ++rowOffset) {
   105	                        // A zero diagonal makes the pivot row unchanged at k.
   106	                        if (rowOffset == offset) continue;
   107	                        const size_t i = begin + rowOffset;
   108	                        relaxRow(distances + i * numNodes + begin,
   109	                                 paths + i * numNodes + begin, snapshot + begin,
   110	                                 pivotColumns[offset * FW_BLOCK_SIZE + rowOffset],
   111	                                 static_cast<unsigned int>(k), width);
   112	                    }
   113	                }
   114	            }
   115	
   116	            // Each worker owns disjoint columns of the pivot rows. Complete
   117	            // their updates and snapshots without any per-pivot barriers.
   118	            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
   119	            for (size_t columnBlock = 0; columnBlock < numBlocks; ++columnBlock) {
   120	                if (columnBlock == block) continue;
   121	                const size_t column = columnBlock * FW_BLOCK_SIZE;
   122	                const size_t count = std::min(FW_BLOCK_SIZE, numNodes - column);
   123	                for (size_t offset = 0; offset < width; ++offset) {
   124	                    const size_t k = begin + offset;
   125	                    unsigned int* snapshot = pivots + offset * numNodes + column;
   126	                    std::copy_n(distances + k * numNodes + column, count, snapshot);
   127	                    for (size_t rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < width; ++rowOffset) {
   128	                        if (rowOffset == offset) continue;
   129	                        const size_t i = begin + rowOffset;
   130	                        relaxRow(distances + i * numNodes + column,
   131	                                 paths + i * numNodes + column, snapshot,
   132	                                 pivotColumns[offset * FW_BLOCK_SIZE + rowOffset],
   133	                                 static_cast<unsigned int>(k), count);
   134	                    }
   135	                }
   136	            }
   137	
   138	            // The barrier above publishes every immutable pivot snapshot.
   139	            // Other rows are now independent: retain each row in cache while
   140	            // applying all pivots in order, preserving distances and paths.
   141	            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
   142	            for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
   143	                if (i >= begin && i < begin + width) continue;
   144	                unsigned int* row = distances + i * numNodes;
   145	                unsigned int* pathRow = paths + i * numNodes;
   146	                for (size_t offset = 0; offset < width; ++offset) {
   147	                    const size_t k = begin + offset;
   148	                    relaxRow(row, pathRow, pivots + offset * numNodes,
   149	                             row[k], static_cast<unsigned int>(k), numNodes);
   150	                }
   151	            }
   152	            // All rows must finish before the next block reads its pivots.
   153	        }
   154	    }
   155	}
   156	
   157	bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {
   158	    // Basic sanity checks
   159	    
   160	    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
   161	    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
   162	        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {
   163	            printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\n", i, i);
   164	            return false;
   165	        }
   166	    }
   167	    
   168	    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]
   169	    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time
   170	    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {
   171	        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {
   172	            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
   173	                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
   174	                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
   175	                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];

 #include <cstring>
 #include <vector>
 
+#include <omp.h>
+
 #include "../common/results_output.hpp"
 
 constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
 constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;
+constexpr size_t FW_BLOCK_SIZE = 16;
 
+inline int matrixThreadCount(const size_t numNodes) noexcept {
+    const size_t blocks = numNodes / FW_BLOCK_SIZE + (numNodes % FW_BLOCK_SIZE != 0);
+    // The pivot-row phase has one independent column tile per block. Avoid
+    // starting more workers than that phase can use, especially on small graphs.
+    return static_cast<int>(std::min(static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()),
+                                     std::max(size_t{1}, blocks)));
+}
+
 // Index calculation for flattened 2D array
 inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
     return j * n + i;

 void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
-    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
-            path[idx2(i, j, numNodes)] = j;
-            path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = i;
+    const int numThreads = matrixThreadCount(numNodes);
+    #pragma omp parallel for num_threads(numThreads) schedule(static) default(none) shared(path, numNodes)
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
+        // Both assignments in the original initialization set each entry to
+        // its source row. Fill each row once, with contiguous stores.
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
+            path[i * numNodes + j] = static_cast<unsigned int>(i);

+inline void relaxRow(unsigned int* row, unsigned int* pathRow,
+                     const unsigned int* pivot, const unsigned int distanceIK,
+                     const unsigned int k, const size_t count) {
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < count; ++j) {
+        const unsigned int candidate = distanceIK + pivot[j];
+        if (candidate < row[j]) {
+            row[j] = candidate;
+            pathRow[j] = k;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
 void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
                    std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                    const size_t numNodes) {
-    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
-    // For each intermediate node k
-    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
-        // For each source node i
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
-            // For each destination node j
-            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
-                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
-                
-                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;
-                
-                if (newDist < distIJ) {
-                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
-                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
+    const size_t numBlocks = numNodes / FW_BLOCK_SIZE + (numNodes % FW_BLOCK_SIZE != 0);
+    const int numThreads = matrixThreadCount(numNodes);
+    // Keep the pivot rows as they appeared at their individual k iterations.
+    // Using their final values after an ordinary blocked closure would change
+    // the original strict-improvement path entries, even with equal distances.
+    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotStorage(FW_BLOCK_SIZE * numNodes);
+    unsigned int pivotColumns[FW_BLOCK_SIZE * FW_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    unsigned int* distances = dist.data();
+    unsigned int* paths = path.data();
+    unsigned int* pivots = pivotStorage.data();
+
+    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(numThreads) default(none) shared(distances, paths, pivots, pivotColumns, numNodes, numBlocks, FW_BLOCK_SIZE)
+    {
+        for (size_t block = 0; block < numBlocks; ++block) {
+            const size_t begin = block * FW_BLOCK_SIZE;
+            const size_t width = std::min(FW_BLOCK_SIZE, numNodes - begin);
+
+            // First process the small diagonal tile in the original k order,
+            // saving both the pivot row and source-to-pivot column at each k.
+            #pragma omp single
+            {
+                for (size_t offset = 0; offset < width; ++offset) {
+                    const size_t k = begin + offset;
+                    const unsigned int* pivot = distances + k * numNodes;
+                    unsigned int* snapshot = pivots + offset * numNodes;
+                    std::copy_n(pivot + begin, width, snapshot + begin);
+                    for (size_t rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < width; ++rowOffset) {
+                        pivotColumns[offset * FW_BLOCK_SIZE + rowOffset] =
+                            distances[(begin + rowOffset) * numNodes + k];
+                    }
+                    for (size_t rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < width; ++rowOffset) {
+                        // A zero diagonal makes the pivot row unchanged at k.
+                        if (rowOffset == offset) continue;
+                        const size_t i = begin + rowOffset;
+                        relaxRow(distances + i * numNodes + begin,
+                                 paths + i * numNodes + begin, snapshot + begin,
+                                 pivotColumns[offset * FW_BLOCK_SIZE + rowOffset],
+                                 static_cast<unsigned int>(k), width);
+                    }
                 }
             }
+
+            // Each worker owns disjoint columns of the pivot rows. Complete
+            // their updates and snapshots without any per-pivot barriers.
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t columnBlock = 0; columnBlock < numBlocks; ++columnBlock) {
+                if (columnBlock == block) continue;
+                const size_t column = columnBlock * FW_BLOCK_SIZE;
+                const size_t count = std::min(FW_BLOCK_SIZE, numNodes - column);
+                for (size_t offset = 0; offset < width; ++offset) {
+                    const size_t k = begin + offset;
+                    unsigned int* snapshot = pivots + offset * numNodes + column;
+                    std::copy_n(distances + k * numNodes + column, count, snapshot);
+                    for (size_t rowOffset = 0; rowOffset < width; ++rowOffset) {
+                        if (rowOffset == offset) continue;
+                        const size_t i = begin + rowOffset;
+                        relaxRow(distances + i * numNodes + column,
+                                 paths + i * numNodes + column, snapshot,
+                                 pivotColumns[offset * FW_BLOCK_SIZE + rowOffset],
+                                 static_cast<unsigned int>(k), count);
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+
+            // The barrier above publishes every immutable pivot snapshot.
+            // Other rows are now independent: retain each row in cache while
+            // applying all pivots in order, preserving distances and paths.
+            #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
+                if (i >= begin && i < begin + width) continue;
+                unsigned int* row = distances + i * numNodes;
+                unsigned int* pathRow = paths + i * numNodes;
+                for (size_t offset = 0; offset < width; ++offset) {
+                    const size_t k = begin + offset;
+                    relaxRow(row, pathRow, pivots + offset * numNodes,
+                             row[k], static_cast<unsigned int>(k), numNodes);
+                }
+            }
+            // All rows must finish before the next block reads its pivots.
         }
     }
 }

codex
Implemented OpenMP with persistent workers, SIMD, and 16-pivot batching. CMake now requires OpenMP; the executable remains `floydwarshall`, with no new project files.

Exact distance and path comparisons passed in 375 cases. The CMake build and ASan/UBSan checks passed.

Measured at 1,024 nodes: **349 ms with 1 thread → 14 ms with 32 threads**, about **25× faster**.
